data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"// Initialize the client\nconst client = new TelnyxRTC({\n  /* Use a JWT to authenticate (recommended) */\n  login_token: login_token,\n  /* or use your Connection credentials */\n  // login: username,\n  // password: password,\n});\n\n// Connect and login\nclient.connect();\n\n// You can call client.disconnect() when you're done.\n// Note: When you call `client.disconnect()` you need to remove all ON event methods you've had attached before.\n\n// Disconnecting and Removing listeners.\n// client.disconnect();\n// client.off('telnyx.ready')\n// \u003ca href=\"/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/docs/ts/classes/telnyxrtc.md#constructor\"\u003eTelnyxRTC#constructor\u003c/a\u003e for all options.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eImportant: You should treat Connection credentials as sensitive data and should not hardcode credentials into your frontend web application. Check out the \u003ca href=\"/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/examples\"\u003eapp examples\u003c/a\u003e for sample code that handles username and password by prompting the user.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTo hear/view calls in the browser, you'll need to specify an HTML media element:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"client.remoteElement = 'remoteMedia';\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eclient\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eremoteElement\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'remoteMedia'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe corresponding HTML:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"\u0026lt;audio id=\u0026quot;remoteMedia\u0026quot; autoplay=\u0026quot;true\u0026quot; /\u0026gt;\n\u0026lt;!-- or for video: --\u0026gt;\n\u0026lt;!-- \u0026lt;video id=\u0026quot;remoteMedia\u0026quot; autoplay=\u0026quot;true\u0026quot; playsinline=\u0026quot;true\u0026quot; /\u0026gt; --\u0026gt;\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e\u0026lt;\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003eaudio\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eid\u003c/span\u003e=\"\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003eremoteMedia\u003c/span\u003e\" \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eautoplay\u003c/span\u003e=\"\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003etrue\u003c/span\u003e\" \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e/\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u0026lt;!-- or for video: --\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u0026lt;!-- \u0026lt;video id=\"remoteMedia\" autoplay=\"true\" playsinline=\"true\" /\u0026gt; --\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003ePer-call \u003ccode\u003eremoteElement\u003c/code\u003e (concurrent calls)\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-per-call-remoteelement-concurrent-calls\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Per-call remoteElement (concurrent calls)\" href=\"#per-call-remoteelement-concurrent-calls\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBy default \u003ccode\u003eclient.remoteElement\u003c/code\u003e is shared across all calls in a session — the\nlast call to connect overwrites the element, and hanging up any call detaches\nthe stream from it. This breaks multi-call scenarios (e.g. one active + one\nheld call).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor concurrent calls in a single client session, assign a \u003cstrong\u003edistinct\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003ccode\u003eremoteElement\u003c/code\u003e per call. Pass it at call creation for outbound calls, or at\n\u003ccode\u003eanswer()\u003c/code\u003e time for inbound calls:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"// Outbound: per-call remoteElement via newCall()\nconst callA = client.newCall({\n  destinationNumber: '18004377950',\n  remoteElement: document.getElementById('remoteMediaA'),\n});\n\n// Inbound: per-call remoteElement via answer()\nclient.on('telnyx.notification', (notification) =\u0026gt; {\n  const { call } = notification;\n  if (call.state === 'ringing') {\n    call.answer({ remoteElement: document.getElementById('remoteMediaB') });\n  }\n});\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// Outbound: per-call remoteElement via newCall()\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003econst\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ecallA\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eclient\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003enewCall\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003edestinationNumber\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'18004377950'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eremoteElement\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003edocument\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003egetElementById\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'remoteMediaA'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// Inbound: per-call remoteElement via answer()\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eclient\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eon\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'telnyx.notification'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003enotification\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003econst\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e call \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003enotification\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eif\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ecall\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003estate\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e===\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'ringing'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ecall\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eanswer\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eremoteElement\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003edocument\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003egetElementById\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'remoteMediaB'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEach call then manages its own remote stream attachment/detachment\nindependently — connecting a second call never disrupts the first call's\nplayout, and hanging up one call only detaches that call's stream. The\nsession-level \u003ccode\u003eclient.remoteElement\u003c/code\u003e remains the fallback default for any call\nthat doesn't specify its own element.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eAnswer-time media and device selection\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-answer-time-media-and-device-selection\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Answer-time media and device selection\" href=\"#answer-time-media-and-device-selection\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eInbound calls accept the same \u003ccode\u003emicId\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003emicLabel\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003espeakerId\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003ecamId\u003c/code\u003e,\n\u003ccode\u003ecamLabel\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eaudio\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003evideo\u003c/code\u003e options as \u003ccode\u003enewCall()\u003c/code\u003e. Apply them when answering,\nalongside per-call elements or custom headers:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"call.answer({\n  micId: selectedMicrophoneId,\n  speakerId: selectedSpeakerId,\n  audio: { echoCancellation: true },\n  remoteElement: document.getElementById('remoteMedia'),\n});\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ecall\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eanswer\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003emicId\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eselectedMicrophoneId\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003espeakerId\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eselectedSpeakerId\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eaudio\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eechoCancellation\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etrue\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eremoteElement\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003edocument\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003egetElementById\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'remoteMedia'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThese overrides apply to the initial call media setup, not a later device switch.\nOmitted or \u003ccode\u003eundefined\u003c/code\u003e fields retain the call's inherited defaults. Explicit\n\u003ccode\u003eaudio: false\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003evideo: false\u003c/code\u003e disables capture for that kind even when a device\nID is stored. A valid existing \u003ccode\u003elocalStream\u003c/code\u003e is reused, not replaced. Client\ndefaults and other calls are unchanged; use distinct remote elements for\nindependent speaker routing across concurrent calls.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSpeaker routing requires browser \u003ccode\u003esetSinkId\u003c/code\u003e support, permission, and a remote\nmedia element. Device discovery may still request permission using a temporary\ndefault-device stream before the selected-device capture. Camera/video options\nuse existing video negotiation and cannot add video to an audio-only offer.\nThese options do not provide automatic hot-plug recovery.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv It returns a \u003ccode\u003ePreCallDiagnosticReport\u003c/code\u003e with stable\nmachine-readable reason/warning codes.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAvailability:\u003c/strong\u003e this family is part of the consolidated pre-call\ndiagnostics work (\u003ca href=\"https://github.com/team-telnyx/webrtc/pull/733\" data-hovercard-type=\"pull_request\" data-hovercard-url=\"/team-telnyx/webrtc/pull/733/hovercard\"\u003eVSDK-412\u003c/a\u003e)\nand is \u003cstrong\u003enot yet available in a published \u003ccode\u003e@telnyx/webrtc\u003c/code\u003e package\u003c/strong\u003e until\nthat work merges and is released. Confirm the installed package version\nbefore relying on these symbols.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"// runPreCall() requires an authenticated client (wait for telnyx.ready).\nclient.on('telnyx.ready', async () =\u0026gt; {\n  const report = await client.runPreCall();\n  console.log(report.verdict); // 'ready' | 'degraded' | 'blocked' | 'permission_denied' | 'inconclusive'\n  for (const reason of report.reasons ?? []) {\n    console.log(reason.code, '-', reason.message);\n  }\n});\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// runPreCall() requires an authenticated client (wait for TelnyxRTC client can be used with an AI assistant by using the \u003ccode\u003eanonymous_login\u003c/code\u003e method. This allows you to authenticate without a SIP connection, making it easier to integrate with AI assistants.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNote:\u003c/strong\u003e In order for anonymous login to work, you have to set \u003ccode\u003etelephony_settings.supports_unauthenticated_web_calls\u003c/code\u003e to \u003ccode\u003etrue\u003c/code\u003e via the API or in the assistant's telephony settings tab in the portal.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"const client = new TelnyxRTC({\n  anonymous_login: {\n    /** Your AI assistant's ID */\n    target_id: 'assistant-UUID',\n    target_type: 'ai_assistant',\n  },\n});\n\nclient.connect();\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003econst\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eclient\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003enew\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eTelnyxRTC\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eanonymous_login\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e/** Your AI assistant's ID */\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etarget_id\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'assistant-UUID'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etarget_type\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'ai_assistant'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eclient\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003econnect\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBy default, anonymous login starts a new AI Assistant conversation. Do not pass\n\u003ccode\u003etarget_params.conversation_id\u003c/code\u003e for application session tracking or as a\ncustomer-generated correlation ID.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003etarget_params.conversation_id\u003c/code\u003e is only for joining an existing Telnyx AI\nconversation:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"const client = new TelnyxRTC({\n  anonymous_login: {\n    target_id: 'assistant-UUID',\n    target_type: 'ai_assistant',\n    target_params: {\n      conversation_id: 'existing-telnyx-conversation-id',\n    },\n  },\n});\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003econst\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eclient\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003enew\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eTelnyxRTC\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eanonymous_login\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etarget_id\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'assistant-UUID'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etarget_type\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'ai_assistant'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etarget_params\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n      \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003econversation_id\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'existing-telnyx-conversation-id'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen \u003ccode\u003econversation_id\u003c/code\u003e is omitted, the TeXML endpoint starts a new conversation\nwith \u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;AIAssistant id=\"...\"\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e. When it is provided, voice-sdk-proxy forwards it\nas the \u003ccode\u003eX-AI-Assistant-Conversation-ID\u003c/code\u003e SIP header and the TeXML endpoint uses\n\u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;AIAssistant join=\"...\"\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e to attach the call to that existing conversation. If\nthe conversation does not exist or does not belong to the caller's\nproject/account, the join fails (for example with AI Assistant error code\n\u003ccode\u003e10007\u003c/code\u003e: \u003ccode\u003eThe conversation does not exist or does not belong to this user\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eMaking Calls to AI Assistants\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-making-calls-to-ai-assistants\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Making Calls to AI Assistants\" href=\"#making-calls-to-ai-assistants\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMaking calls to the AI assistant is similar to making calls with a SIP connection. You can use the \u003ccode\u003enewCall\u003c/code\u003e method to initiate a call, the destination number can be left blank.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"const call = client.newCall({\n  destinationNumber: '',\n  remoteElement: 'remoteElement',\n  /** other call options. */\n});\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003econst\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ecall\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eclient\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003enewCall\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003edestinationNumber\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e''\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eremoteElement\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'remoteElement'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e/** other call options. */\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIts recommended that you set the preferred codec to \u003ccode\u003eopus\u003c/code\u003e by passing the \u003ccode\u003epreferred_codecs\u003c/code\u003e option to the \u003ccode\u003enewCall\u003c/code\u003e method when calling AI assistants.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"const allCodecs = RTCRtpReceiver.getCapabilities('audio').codecs;\nconst opusCodec = allCodecs.find((c) =\u0026gt;\n  c.mimeType.toLowerCase().includes('opus')\n);\n\nclient.newCall({\n  destinationNumber: '',\n  preferred_codecs: [opusCodec],\n});\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003econst\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eallCodecs\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eRTCRtpReceiver\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003egetCapabilities\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'audio'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ecodecs\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003econst\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eopusCodec\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eallCodecs\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003efind\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ec\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e class=\"pl-c1\"\u003emimeType\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003etoLowerCase\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eincludes\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'opus'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eclient\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003enewCall\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003edestinationNumber\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e''\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003epreferred_codecs\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e[\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eopusCodec\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e]\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan dir=\"auto\"\u003eReceiving and Responding to AI Conversation Events\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-receiving-and-responding-to-ai-conversation-events\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Receiving and Responding to AI Conversation Events\" href=\"#receiving-and-responding-to-ai-conversation-events\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen the AI backend wants the client to execute a tool, it sends a \u003ccode\u003efunction_call\u003c/code\u003e item via the \u003ccode\u003etelnyx.ai.conversation\u003c/code\u003e event. Your application executes the tool and sends the result back using \u003ccode\u003ecall.sendAIConversationMessage()\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"client.on('telnyx.ai.conversation', (event) =\u0026gt; {\n  // Check if the message contains a function_call item\n  if (\n    event.params.type === 'conversation.item.created' \u0026amp;\u0026amp;\n    event.params.item?.type === 'function_call'\n  ) {\n    const { call_id, name, arguments: argsJson } = event.params.item;\n\n    // Execute the client-side tool\n    const result = executeTool(name, JSON.parse(argsJson));\n\n    // Send the result back to the AI backend\n    call.sendAIConversationMessage({\n      type: 'function_call_output',\n      call_id,\n      output: JSON.stringify(result),\n    });\n  }\n});\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eclient\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eon\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'telnyx.ai.conversation'\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eevent\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// Check if the message contains a function_call item\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eif\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eevent\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eparams\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etype\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e===\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'conversation.item.created'\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\u0026amp;\u0026amp;\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eevent\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eparams\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eitem\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e?.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etype\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e===\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e'function_call'\u003c/span\u003e\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003econst\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e{\u003c/span\u003e call_id\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e name\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003earguments\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eargsJson\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e}\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eevent\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eparams\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eitem\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e;\u003c/span\u003e\n\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e// Execute the client-side tool\u003c/span\u003e\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003econst\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eresult\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eexecuteTool\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ename\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan <p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/3730c9c94420f0a07d7994c939a3c0fbbcebc48a34cac4a45927f31d1ecda128/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f6e706d2f762f4074656c6e79782f776562727463"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/3730c9c94420f0a07d7994c939a3c0fbbcebc48a34cac4a45927f31d1ecda128/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f6e706d2f762f4074656c6e79782f776562727463" alt="npm (scoped)" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/npm/v/@telnyx/webrtc" style="max-width: 100%;"></a> <a href="https://joinslack.telnyx.com/" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/06139314ccb7b34f9b6e447f18d1deafb8bcc0ab00d8451469615242ae5e9063/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f6a6f696e2d736c61636b2d696e666f6d6174696f6e616c" alt="Join Slack" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/badge/join-slack-infomational" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Summary</h2><a id="user-content-summary" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Summary" href="#summary"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The Telnyx WebRTC Client provides all the functionality you need to start making voice &amp; video calls from a browser.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="#Installation">Installation</a></li>
<li><a href="#Usage">Usage</a></li>
<li><a href="#Examples">Examples</a>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="#vanilla-javascript">Vanilla JavaScript</a></li>
<li><a href="#reactjs">React.js</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><a href="/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/docs/error-handling.md">Error Handling</a></li>
<li><a href="/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/docs/pre-call-diagnostics.md">Pre-Call Diagnostics</a></li>
<li><a href="#Browser-support">Browser support</a></li>
<li><a href="#Development">Development</a></li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h2><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Install the package with:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install @telnyx/webrtc --save"><pre class="notranslate"><code>npm install @telnyx/webrtc --save
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">As long as you can import npm packages with a bundler like <a href="https://webpack.js.org/" rel="nofollow">Webpack</a>, you're ready to import <code>TelnyxRTC</code> and begin:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import { TelnyxRTC } from '@telnyx/webrtc';"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-v">TelnyxRTC</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'@telnyx/webrtc'</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Usage</h2><a id="user-content-usage" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Usage" href="#usage"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To initialize the WebRTC client, you'll need to authenticate using a Telnyx SIP Connection. Follow our <a href="https://developers.telnyx.com/docs/v2/webrtc/quickstart" rel="nofollow">quickstart guide</a> to create <strong>JWTs</strong> (JSON Web Tokens) to authenticate. You can also authenticate directly with the SIP Connection <code>username</code> and <code>password</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Client</h3><a id="user-content-client" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Client" href="#client"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Initialize the client
const client = new TelnyxRTC({
  /* Use a JWT to authenticate (recommended) */
  login_token: login_token,
  /* or use your Connection credentials */
  // login: username,
  // password: password,
});

// Connect and login
client.connect();

// You can call client.disconnect() when you're done.
// Note: When you call `client.disconnect()` you need to remove all ON event methods you've had attached before.

// Disconnecting and Removing listeners.
// client.disconnect();
// client.off('telnyx.ready')
// client.off('telnyx.notification');"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Initialize the client</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">TelnyxRTC</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">/* Use a JWT to authenticate (recommended) */</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">login_token</span>: <span class="pl-s1">login_token</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c">/* or use your Connection credentials */</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// login: username,</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// password: password,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Connect and login</span>
<span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">connect</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// You can call client.disconnect() when you're done.</span>
<span class="pl-c">// Note: When you call `client.disconnect()` you need to remove all ON event methods you've had attached before.</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Disconnecting and Removing listeners.</span>
<span class="pl-c">// client.disconnect();</span>
<span class="pl-c">// client.off('telnyx.ready')</span>
<span class="pl-c">// client.off('telnyx.notification');</span></pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">See ON <a href="https://github.com/team-telnyx/webrtc/tree/main/packages/js#events">Events</a></p>
</blockquote>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/docs/ts/classes/telnyxrtc.md#constructor">TelnyxRTC#constructor</a> for all options.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Important: You should treat Connection credentials as sensitive data and should not hardcode credentials into your frontend web application. Check out the <a href="/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/examples">app examples</a> for sample code that handles username and password by prompting the user.</p>
<p dir="auto">To hear/view calls in the browser, you'll need to specify an HTML media element:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="client.remoteElement = 'remoteMedia';"><pre><span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">remoteElement</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">'remoteMedia'</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The corresponding HTML:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;audio id=&quot;remoteMedia&quot; autoplay=&quot;true&quot; /&gt;
&lt;!-- or for video: --&gt;
&lt;!-- &lt;video id=&quot;remoteMedia&quot; autoplay=&quot;true&quot; playsinline=&quot;true&quot; /&gt; --&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">audio</span> <span class="pl-c1">id</span>="<span class="pl-s">remoteMedia</span>" <span class="pl-c1">autoplay</span>="<span class="pl-s">true</span>" <span class="pl-kos">/&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- or for video: --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- &lt;video id="remoteMedia" autoplay="true" playsinline="true" /&gt; --&gt;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Per-call <code>remoteElement</code> (concurrent calls)</h4><a id="user-content-per-call-remoteelement-concurrent-calls" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Per-call remoteElement (concurrent calls)" href="#per-call-remoteelement-concurrent-calls"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">By default <code>client.remoteElement</code> is shared across all calls in a session — the
last call to connect overwrites the element, and hanging up any call detaches
the stream from it. This breaks multi-call scenarios (e.g. one active + one
held call).</p>
<p dir="auto">For concurrent calls in a single client session, assign a <strong>distinct</strong>
<code>remoteElement</code> per call. Pass it at call creation for outbound calls, or at
<code>answer()</code> time for inbound calls:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Outbound: per-call remoteElement via newCall()
const callA = client.newCall({
  destinationNumber: '18004377950',
  remoteElement: document.getElementById('remoteMediaA'),
});

// Inbound: per-call remoteElement via answer()
client.on('telnyx.notification', (notification) =&gt; {
  const { call } = notification;
  if (call.state === 'ringing') {
    call.answer({ remoteElement: document.getElementById('remoteMediaB') });
  }
});"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Outbound: per-call remoteElement via newCall()</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">callA</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">newCall</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">destinationNumber</span>: <span class="pl-s">'18004377950'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">remoteElement</span>: <span class="pl-smi">document</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">getElementById</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'remoteMediaA'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Inbound: per-call remoteElement via answer()</span>
<span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">on</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'telnyx.notification'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">notification</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> call <span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">notification</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">call</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">state</span> <span class="pl-c1">===</span> <span class="pl-s">'ringing'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">call</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">answer</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">remoteElement</span>: <span class="pl-smi">document</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">getElementById</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'remoteMediaB'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Each call then manages its own remote stream attachment/detachment
independently — connecting a second call never disrupts the first call's
playout, and hanging up one call only detaches that call's stream. The
session-level <code>client.remoteElement</code> remains the fallback default for any call
that doesn't specify its own element.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Answer-time media and device selection</h4><a id="user-content-answer-time-media-and-device-selection" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Answer-time media and device selection" href="#answer-time-media-and-device-selection"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Inbound calls accept the same <code>micId</code>, <code>micLabel</code>, <code>speakerId</code>, <code>camId</code>,
<code>camLabel</code>, <code>audio</code>, and <code>video</code> options as <code>newCall()</code>. Apply them when answering,
alongside per-call elements or custom headers:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="call.answer({
  micId: selectedMicrophoneId,
  speakerId: selectedSpeakerId,
  audio: { echoCancellation: true },
  remoteElement: document.getElementById('remoteMedia'),
});"><pre><span class="pl-s1">call</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">answer</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">micId</span>: <span class="pl-s1">selectedMicrophoneId</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">speakerId</span>: <span class="pl-s1">selectedSpeakerId</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">audio</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-c1">echoCancellation</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">remoteElement</span>: <span class="pl-smi">document</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">getElementById</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'remoteMedia'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">These overrides apply to the initial call media setup, not a later device switch.
Omitted or <code>undefined</code> fields retain the call's inherited defaults. Explicit
<code>audio: false</code> or <code>video: false</code> disables capture for that kind even when a device
ID is stored. A valid existing <code>localStream</code> is reused, not replaced. Client
defaults and other calls are unchanged; use distinct remote elements for
independent speaker routing across concurrent calls.</p>
<p dir="auto">Speaker routing requires browser <code>setSinkId</code> support, permission, and a remote
media element. Device discovery may still request permission using a temporary
default-device stream before the selected-device capture. Camera/video options
use existing video negotiation and cannot add video to an audio-only offer.
These options do not provide automatic hot-plug recovery.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Events</h3><a id="user-content-events" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Events" href="#events"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Create a variable to track the current call
let activeCall;

// Attach event listeners
client
  .on('telnyx.ready', () =&gt; console.log('ready to call'))
  .on('telnyx.error', () =&gt; console.log('error'))
  // Events are fired on both session and call updates
  // ex: when the session has been established
  // ex: when there's an incoming call
  .on('telnyx.notification', (notification) =&gt; {
    if (notification.type === 'callUpdate') {
      activeCall = notification.call;
    }
  });"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Create a variable to track the current call</span>
<span class="pl-k">let</span> <span class="pl-s1">activeCall</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Attach event listeners</span>
<span class="pl-s1">client</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">on</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'telnyx.ready'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'ready to call'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">on</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'telnyx.error'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'error'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Events are fired on both session and call updates</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// ex: when the session has been established</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// ex: when there's an incoming call</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">on</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'telnyx.notification'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">notification</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">notification</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">===</span> <span class="pl-s">'callUpdate'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-s1">activeCall</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">notification</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">call</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/docs/ts/classes/telnyxrtc.md#on">TelnyxRTC.on</a> for all events.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Calls</h3><a id="user-content-calls" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Calls" href="#calls"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To initiate an outgoing call:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const call = client.newCall({
  // Destination is required and can be a phone number or SIP URI
  destinationNumber: '18004377950',
  callerNumber: '‬155531234567',
});"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">call</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">newCall</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Destination is required and can be a phone number or SIP URI</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">destinationNumber</span>: <span class="pl-s">'18004377950'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">callerNumber</span>: <span class="pl-s">'‬155531234567'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">To enable video when calling:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const videoCall = client.newCall({
  destinationNumber: 'sip:example@sip.example.com',
  video: true,
});

// And in your HTML, replace the audio element with video.
//  &lt;video id=&quot;remoteMedia&quot; autoplay=&quot;true&quot; playsinline=&quot;true&quot; /&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">videoCall</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">newCall</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">destinationNumber</span>: <span class="pl-s">'sip:example@sip.example.com'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">video</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// And in your HTML, replace the audio element with video.</span>
<span class="pl-c">//  &lt;video id="remoteMedia" autoplay="true" playsinline="true" /&gt;</span></pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/docs/ts/classes/telnyxrtc.md#newCall">TelnyxRTC.newCall</a> for all options.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">To answer an incoming call:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="client.on('telnyx.notification', (notification) =&gt; {
  const call = notification.call;

  if (notification.type === 'callUpdate' &amp;&amp; call.state === 'ringing') {
    call.answer();
  }
});"><pre><span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">on</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'telnyx.notification'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">notification</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">call</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">notification</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">call</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

  <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">notification</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">===</span> <span class="pl-s">'callUpdate'</span> <span class="pl-c1">&amp;&amp;</span> <span class="pl-s1">call</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">state</span> <span class="pl-c1">===</span> <span class="pl-s">'ringing'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">call</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">answer</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Both the outgoing and incoming <code>Call</code> instance has methods that can be hooked up to your UI:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Hangup or reject an incoming call
call.hangup();

// Send digits and keypresses
call.dtmf('1234');

// Call states that can be toggled
call.hold();
call.muteAudio();"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Hangup or reject an incoming call</span>
<span class="pl-s1">call</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">hangup</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Send digits and keypresses</span>
<span class="pl-s1">call</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">dtmf</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'1234'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// Call states that can be toggled</span>
<span class="pl-s1">call</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">hold</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-s1">call</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">muteAudio</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/docs/ts/classes/call.md#methods">Call#methods</a> for all methods.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Debugging</h3><a id="user-content-debugging" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Debugging" href="#debugging"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">In order to have a better idea on what is going on under the hood you can gather webrtc metrics for a call:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const call = client.newCall({
  // Destination is required and can be a phone number or SIP URI
  destinationNumber: '18004377950',
  callerNumber: '‬155531234567',
  debug: true // Default is false,
  debugOutput: 'socket' // Possible values are 'socket' | 'file'
});

// The debug dump is set to be sent to telnyx by default, if you want to save the debug data to disk
// You can change the debugOutput option to 'file'"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">call</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">newCall</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Destination is required and can be a phone number or SIP URI</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">destinationNumber</span>: <span class="pl-s">'18004377950'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">callerNumber</span>: <span class="pl-s">'‬155531234567'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">debug</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span> <span class="pl-c">// Default is false,</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">debugOutput</span>: <span class="pl-s">'socket'</span> <span class="pl-c">// Possible values are 'socket' | 'file'</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-c">// The debug dump is set to be sent to telnyx by default, if you want to save the debug data to disk</span>
<span class="pl-c">// You can change the debugOutput option to 'file'</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">In-Call Quality Metrics</h3><a id="user-content-in-call-quality-metrics" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: In-Call Quality Metrics" href="#in-call-quality-metrics"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To be able to get the in-call quality metrics you have to first enable debugging for the call and then listen for the <code>telnyx.stats.frame</code> event.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const call = client.newCall({
  // Destination is required and can be a phone number or SIP URI
  destinationNumber: '18004377950',
  debug: true, // it is required to enable debugging for the call,
});

client.on('telnyx.stats.frame', (stats) =&gt; {
  console.log(stats);
});"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">call</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">newCall</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Destination is required and can be a phone number or SIP URI</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">destinationNumber</span>: <span class="pl-s">'18004377950'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">debug</span>: <span class="pl-c1">true</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c">// it is required to enable debugging for the call,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">on</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'telnyx.stats.frame'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">stats</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">stats</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Pre-Call Diagnostics</h3><a id="user-content-pre-call-diagnostics" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Pre-Call Diagnostics" href="#pre-call-diagnostics"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The SDK provides two pre-call diagnostic APIs. See the
<a href="/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/docs/pre-call-diagnostics.md">Pre-Call Diagnostics guide</a> for the full
contract, option defaults, report interpretation, and privacy/safe-sharing
guidance.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>PreCallDiagnostic</code> (new — <code>client.runPreCall()</code> family)</h4><a id="user-content-precalldiagnostic-new--clientrunprecall-family" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: PreCallDiagnostic (new — client.runPreCall() family)" href="#precalldiagnostic-new--clientrunprecall-family"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>PreCallDiagnostic</code> family (<code>client.runPreCall()</code>,
<code>client.runNetworkCheck()</code>, <code>client.runMicrophoneCheck()</code>) provides
structured verdicts, per-ICE-server testing, and microphone checks with
optional recording. It returns a <code>PreCallDiagnosticReport</code> with stable
machine-readable reason/warning codes.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Availability:</strong> this family is part of the consolidated pre-call
diagnostics work (<a href="https://github.com/team-telnyx/webrtc/pull/733" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/team-telnyx/webrtc/pull/733/hovercard">VSDK-412</a>)
and is <strong>not yet available in a published <code>@telnyx/webrtc</code> package</strong> until
that work merges and is released. Confirm the installed package version
before relying on these symbols.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// runPreCall() requires an authenticated client (wait for telnyx.ready).
client.on('telnyx.ready', async () =&gt; {
  const report = await client.runPreCall();
  console.log(report.verdict); // 'ready' | 'degraded' | 'blocked' | 'permission_denied' | 'inconclusive'
  for (const reason of report.reasons ?? []) {
    console.log(reason.code, '-', reason.message);
  }
});"><pre><span class="pl-c">// runPreCall() requires an authenticated client (wait for telnyx.ready).</span>
<span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">on</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'telnyx.ready'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">report</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">await</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">runPreCall</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">report</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">verdict</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span> <span class="pl-c">// 'ready' | 'degraded' | 'blocked' | 'permission_denied' | 'inconclusive'</span>
  <span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">reason</span> <span class="pl-k">of</span> <span class="pl-s1">report</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">reasons</span> <span class="pl-c1">??</span> <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">reason</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">code</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">'-'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s1">reason</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">message</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>PreCallDiagnosis</code> (legacy — static <code>PreCallDiagnosis.run()</code>)</h4><a id="user-content-precalldiagnosis-legacy--static-precalldiagnosisrun" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: PreCallDiagnosis (legacy — static PreCallDiagnosis.run())" href="#precalldiagnosis-legacy--static-precalldiagnosisrun"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The legacy <code>PreCallDiagnosis.run()</code> method remains available and unchanged.
It creates its own <code>TelnyxRTC</code> from the provided credentials, places a
single diagnostic call, and returns a <code>Report</code> with ICE candidate stats,
selected pair, and summary MOS/quality.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import { PreCallDiagnosis } from '@telnyx/webrtc';

PreCallDiagnosis.run({
  credentials: {
    login: clientOptions.login,
    password: clientOptions.password,
    loginToken: clientOptions.login_token,
  },
  texMLApplicationNumber: '+1-240-775-8982',
})
  .then((report) =&gt; {
    console.log(report);
  })
  .catch((error) =&gt; {
    console.error(error);
  });"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-v">PreCallDiagnosis</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'@telnyx/webrtc'</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-v">PreCallDiagnosis</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">run</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">credentials</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">login</span>: <span class="pl-s1">clientOptions</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">login</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">password</span>: <span class="pl-s1">clientOptions</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">password</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">loginToken</span>: <span class="pl-s1">clientOptions</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">login_token</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">texMLApplicationNumber</span>: <span class="pl-s">'+1-240-775-8982'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">then</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">report</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">log</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">report</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">catch</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">error</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-smi">console</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">error</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">error</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Do not confuse the names.</strong> <code>PreCallDiagnostic</code> (the new class/report
family) and <code>PreCallDiagnosis</code> (the legacy class) are different exports.
The new family is not a drop-in replacement for the legacy API — their
report shapes are not structurally equivalent.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Setting Preferred Codec</h3><a id="user-content-setting-preferred-codec" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Setting Preferred Codec" href="#setting-preferred-codec"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can pass <code>preferred_codecs</code> to the <code>newCall</code> method to set codec preference during the call.</p>
