<p dir="auto"><code>preferred_codecs</code> is a sub-array of the codecs returned by <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/RTCRtpReceiver/getCapabilities_static#codecs" rel="nofollow">RTCRtpReceiver.getCapabilities('audio')</a></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const allCodecs = RTCRtpReceiver.getCapabilities('audio').codecs;

const PCMACodec = allCodecs.find((c) =&gt;
  c.mimeType.toLowerCase().includes('pcma')
);

client.newCall({
  destinationNumber: '123',
  preferred_codecs: [PCMACodec],
});"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">allCodecs</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">RTCRtpReceiver</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">getCapabilities</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'audio'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">codecs</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-v">PCMACodec</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">allCodecs</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">find</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">c</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">c</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">mimeType</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toLowerCase</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">includes</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'pcma'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">newCall</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">destinationNumber</span>: <span class="pl-s">'123'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">preferred_codecs</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-v">PCMACodec</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Getting the Registration State</h3><a id="user-content-getting-the-registration-state" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Getting the Registration State" href="#getting-the-registration-state"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To retrieve the registration state from the server gateway you can
use <code>client.getIsRegistered</code> method</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="client.getIsRegistered().then(isRegistered =&gt; {...})"><pre><span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">getIsRegistered</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">then</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">isRegistered</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>...<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Anonymous Login for AI assistant</h3><a id="user-content-anonymous-login-for-ai-assistant" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Anonymous Login for AI assistant" href="#anonymous-login-for-ai-assistant"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The TelnyxRTC client can be used with an AI assistant by using the <code>anonymous_login</code> method. This allows you to authenticate without a SIP connection, making it easier to integrate with AI assistants.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> In order for anonymous login to work, you have to set <code>telephony_settings.supports_unauthenticated_web_calls</code> to <code>true</code> via the API or in the assistant's telephony settings tab in the portal.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const client = new TelnyxRTC({
  anonymous_login: {
    /** Your AI assistant's ID */
    target_id: 'assistant-UUID',
    target_type: 'ai_assistant',
  },
});

client.connect();"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">TelnyxRTC</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">anonymous_login</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c">/** Your AI assistant's ID */</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">target_id</span>: <span class="pl-s">'assistant-UUID'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">target_type</span>: <span class="pl-s">'ai_assistant'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">connect</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">By default, anonymous login starts a new AI Assistant conversation. Do not pass
<code>target_params.conversation_id</code> for application session tracking or as a
customer-generated correlation ID.</p>
<p dir="auto"><code>target_params.conversation_id</code> is only for joining an existing Telnyx AI
conversation:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const client = new TelnyxRTC({
  anonymous_login: {
    target_id: 'assistant-UUID',
    target_type: 'ai_assistant',
    target_params: {
      conversation_id: 'existing-telnyx-conversation-id',
    },
  },
});"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">TelnyxRTC</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">anonymous_login</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">target_id</span>: <span class="pl-s">'assistant-UUID'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">target_type</span>: <span class="pl-s">'ai_assistant'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">target_params</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-c1">conversation_id</span>: <span class="pl-s">'existing-telnyx-conversation-id'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">When <code>conversation_id</code> is omitted, the TeXML endpoint starts a new conversation
with <code>&lt;AIAssistant id="..."&gt;</code>. When it is provided, voice-sdk-proxy forwards it
as the <code>X-AI-Assistant-Conversation-ID</code> SIP header and the TeXML endpoint uses
<code>&lt;AIAssistant join="..."&gt;</code> to attach the call to that existing conversation. If
the conversation does not exist or does not belong to the caller's
project/account, the join fails (for example with AI Assistant error code
<code>10007</code>: <code>The conversation does not exist or does not belong to this user</code>).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Making Calls to AI Assistants</h3><a id="user-content-making-calls-to-ai-assistants" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Making Calls to AI Assistants" href="#making-calls-to-ai-assistants"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Making calls to the AI assistant is similar to making calls with a SIP connection. You can use the <code>newCall</code> method to initiate a call, the destination number can be left blank.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const call = client.newCall({
  destinationNumber: '',
  remoteElement: 'remoteElement',
  /** other call options. */
});"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">call</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">newCall</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">destinationNumber</span>: <span class="pl-s">''</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">remoteElement</span>: <span class="pl-s">'remoteElement'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c">/** other call options. */</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Its recommended that you set the preferred codec to <code>opus</code> by passing the <code>preferred_codecs</code> option to the <code>newCall</code> method when calling AI assistants.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="const allCodecs = RTCRtpReceiver.getCapabilities('audio').codecs;
const opusCodec = allCodecs.find((c) =&gt;
  c.mimeType.toLowerCase().includes('opus')
);

client.newCall({
  destinationNumber: '',
  preferred_codecs: [opusCodec],
});"><pre><span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">allCodecs</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">RTCRtpReceiver</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">getCapabilities</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'audio'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">codecs</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">opusCodec</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">allCodecs</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">find</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">c</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">c</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">mimeType</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">toLowerCase</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">includes</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'opus'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">newCall</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">destinationNumber</span>: <span class="pl-s">''</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">preferred_codecs</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s1">opusCodec</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Receiving and Responding to AI Conversation Events</h3><a id="user-content-receiving-and-responding-to-ai-conversation-events" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Receiving and Responding to AI Conversation Events" href="#receiving-and-responding-to-ai-conversation-events"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When the AI backend wants the client to execute a tool, it sends a <code>function_call</code> item via the <code>telnyx.ai.conversation</code> event. Your application executes the tool and sends the result back using <code>call.sendAIConversationMessage()</code>.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="client.on('telnyx.ai.conversation', (event) =&gt; {
  // Check if the message contains a function_call item
  if (
    event.params.type === 'conversation.item.created' &amp;&amp;
    event.params.item?.type === 'function_call'
  ) {
    const { call_id, name, arguments: argsJson } = event.params.item;

    // Execute the client-side tool
    const result = executeTool(name, JSON.parse(argsJson));

    // Send the result back to the AI backend
    call.sendAIConversationMessage({
      type: 'function_call_output',
      call_id,
      output: JSON.stringify(result),
    });
  }
});"><pre><span class="pl-s1">client</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">on</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'telnyx.ai.conversation'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">event</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// Check if the message contains a function_call item</span>
  <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">event</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">params</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">===</span> <span class="pl-s">'conversation.item.created'</span> <span class="pl-c1">&amp;&amp;</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">event</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">params</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">item</span><span class="pl-kos">?.</span><span class="pl-c1">type</span> <span class="pl-c1">===</span> <span class="pl-s">'function_call'</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> call_id<span class="pl-kos">,</span> name<span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">arguments</span>: <span class="pl-s1">argsJson</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">event</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">params</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">item</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

    <span class="pl-c">// Execute the client-side tool</span>
    <span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">result</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">executeTool</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">name</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-c1">JSON</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">parse</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">argsJson</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>

    <span class="pl-c">// Send the result back to the AI backend</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">call</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">sendAIConversationMessage</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-c1">type</span>: <span class="pl-s">'function_call_output'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
      call_id<span class="pl-kos">,</span>
      <span class="pl-c1">output</span>: <span class="pl-c1">JSON</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">stringify</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">result</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>call_id</code> from the <code>function_call</code> must match the <code>call_id</code> in the <code>function_call_output</code> — this is how the backend correlates the request with the response.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/docs/ts/classes/Call.md#sendaiconversationmessage">Call#sendAIConversationMessage</a> for the full API reference.</p>
</blockquote>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Examples</h2><a id="user-content-examples" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Examples" href="#examples"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We've included a few <a href="https://github.com/team-telnyx/webrtc-examples/tree/main/js">examples in vanilla JavaScript (ES6) and React</a> to help you get started.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><em>Looking for more examples in React? Check out our React client packages:</em></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/react-client">@telnyx/react-client</a></li>
</ul>
</blockquote>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Browser support</h2><a id="user-content-browser-support" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Browser support" href="#browser-support"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The following table indicates the browsers supported by TelnyxRTC.</p>
<p dir="auto">We support the most recent (N) versions of these browsers unless otherwise indicated.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th></th>
<th>Chrome</th>
<th>Firefox</th>
<th>Safari</th>
<th>Edge</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Android</td>
<td>[-]</td>
<td>[-]</td>
<td>[ ]</td>
<td>[ ]</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>iOS</td>
<td>[ ]</td>
<td>[ ]</td>
<td>[x]</td>
<td>[ ]</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Linux</td>
<td>[x]</td>
<td>[-]</td>
<td>[ ]</td>
<td>[ ]</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>MacOS</td>
<td>[x]</td>
<td>[-]</td>
<td>[x]</td>
<td>[-]</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Windows</td>
<td>[x]</td>
<td>[-]</td>
<td>[ ]</td>
<td>[-]</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">[x] supports audio and video</p>
<p dir="auto">[-] supports only audio</p>
<p dir="auto">[ ] not supported</p>
<p dir="auto">To extend support to other browsers, install and import <a href="https://www.npmjs.com/package/webrtc-adapter" rel="nofollow">webrtc-adapter</a> before importing <code>TelnyxRTC</code>. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import 'webrtc-adapter';
import { TelnyxRTC } from '@telnyx/webrtc';"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s">'webrtc-adapter'</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-v">TelnyxRTC</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'@telnyx/webrtc'</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">To check whether your browser supports TelnyxRTC, use <a href="/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/docs/ts/classes/telnyxrtc.md#webRTCInfo"><code>TelnyxRTC.webRTCInfo</code></a>.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Development</h2><a id="user-content-development" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Development" href="#development"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><em><strong>Requirement</strong> Node v11.15.0 or later</em></p>
<p dir="auto">This library is written in <a href="https://www.typescriptlang.org/" rel="nofollow">TypeScript</a> to define a clear API with optional typechecking benefits.</p>
<p dir="auto">To contribute, clone this repo and install locally:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install"><pre class="notranslate"><code>npm install
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Afterwards, you're ready to make changes to files in <code>src</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">To run all tests:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm test"><pre class="notranslate"><code>npm test
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Documentation</h3><a id="user-content-documentation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Documentation" href="#documentation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="/team-telnyx/webrtc/blob/main/packages/js/docs/ts">TypeScript documentation</a> is automatically generated from TSDoc-style comments on merge to <code>main</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Only code symbols with a symbol-level TSDoc comment will appear in the docs. Use <code>@category</code> to group symbols. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// `PublicUseModule` will appear in docs due to the class-level TSDoc comment.
/**
 * A module for public consumption
 * @category Public Modules
 */
class PublicUseModule {
  // `getSomething` WILL appear in docs because
  // there is a TSDoc comment
  /**
   * Gets something.
   */
  getSomething() {}
  // `getSomethingElse` will NOT appear in docs
  // because of the `@ignore` tag
  /**
   * Adds another thing
   * @ignore
   */
  getSomethingElse() {}
  // `addAnotherThing` will NOT appear in docs
  // because there is no TSDoc comment
  addAnotherThing() {}
  // `updateSomething` will NOT appear in docs
  // because of the `private` keyword
  private updateSomething() {}
  // `deleteSomething` will NOT appear in docs
  // because of the `protected` keyword
  protected deleteSomething() {}
}

// `InternalUseModule` will NOT appear in docs,
// even if its members are documented, because
// there is no class-level TSDoc comment.
class InternalUseModule {
  doThis() {}
  doThat() {}
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// `PublicUseModule` will appear in docs due to the class-level TSDoc comment.</span>
<span class="pl-c">/**</span>
<span class="pl-c"> * A module for public consumption</span>
<span class="pl-c"> * <span class="pl-k">@category</span> Public Modules</span>
<span class="pl-c"> */</span>
<span class="pl-k">class</span> <span class="pl-smi">PublicUseModule</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// `getSomething` WILL appear in docs because</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// there is a TSDoc comment</span>
  <span class="pl-c">/**</span>
<span class="pl-c">   * Gets something.</span>
<span class="pl-c">   */</span>
  <span class="pl-en">getSomething</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// `getSomethingElse` will NOT appear in docs</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// because of the `@ignore` tag</span>
  <span class="pl-c">/**</span>
<span class="pl-c">   * Adds another thing</span>
<span class="pl-c">   * <span class="pl-k">@ignore</span></span>
<span class="pl-c">   */</span>
  <span class="pl-en">getSomethingElse</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// `addAnotherThing` will NOT appear in docs</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// because there is no TSDoc comment</span>
  <span class="pl-en">addAnotherThing</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// `updateSomething` will NOT appear in docs</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// because of the `private` keyword</span>
  <span class="pl-k">private</span> <span class="pl-en">updateSomething</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// `deleteSomething` will NOT appear in docs</span>
  <span class="pl-c">// because of the `protected` keyword</span>
  <span class="pl-k">protected</span> <span class="pl-en">deleteSomething</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span>

<span class="pl-c">// `InternalUseModule` will NOT appear in docs,</span>
<span class="pl-c">// even if its members are documented, because</span>
<span class="pl-c">// there is no class-level TSDoc comment.</span>
<span class="pl-k">class</span> <span class="pl-smi">InternalUseModule</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-en">doThis</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-en">doThat</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If you've added comments and still do not see documentation as expected, check the <code>typedocOptions.exclude</code> config in <code>tsconfig.json</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Supported tags</h4><a id="user-content-supported-tags" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Supported tags" href="#supported-tags"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">In addition to the tags <a href="https://typedoc.org/guides/doccomments/#supported-tags" rel="nofollow">supported by Typedoc</a>, we use <code>apialias</code>, <code>example</code>/<code>examples</code> and <code>internalnote</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><em>Note: <code>@link</code> is not supported in public-facing documentation at this time.</em></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h5 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>@apialias</code></h5><a id="user-content-apialias" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: @apialias" href="#apialias"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use <code>apialias</code> to display a different name in public documentation.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/**
 * @apialias PublicObject
 */
interface IPublicObject {}"><pre><span class="pl-c">/**</span>
<span class="pl-c"> * <span class="pl-k">@apialias</span> PublicObject</span>
<span class="pl-c"> */</span>
<span class="pl-k">interface</span> <span class="pl-smi">IPublicObject</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>

    <path d="M3.72 3.72a.75.75 0 0 1 1.06 0L8 6.94l3.22-3.22a.749.749 0 0 1 1.275.326.749.749 0 0 1-.215.734L9.06 8l3.22 3.22a.749.749 0 0 1-.326 1.275.749.749 0 0 1-.734-.215L8 9.06l-3.22 3.22a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042L6.94 8 3.72 4.78a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.06Z"></path>
</svg>
      </button>
      <div class="octocat-spinner tmp-my-6 js-details-dialog-spinner"></div>
    </details-dialog>
  </details>
</template>
